{"id":"b313da89-8ac3-43fb-ad5b-e9f76a6050f2","icon":null,"color":"#bec2c8","name":"drain-cycle supervises; the pack owns the workflow","summary":"Re-scope drain-cycle to a thin vendor-agnostic supervisor (spawn, guardrails, halt, resume, grade); the intra-issue workflow moves into the Shaper pack. Third of three in the lifecycle expansion.","description":"**Goal:** For Anton (single user), make unattended cycle drains run with the supervisor owning only process concerns — spawn, guardrails, halt, resume, grade — so any vendor's worker can follow the pack's workflow prose unchanged.\n\n**Key results:**\n\n**KR1 (stretch)** — a full cycle drains to completion with the worker prompt reduced to issue context + a single skill pointer (zero inlined workflow steps), at the execution initiative's quality bar (no manual fix commits) *(bet)*\n\n* baseline: `drain_cycle/prompt.py` inlines \\~30 lines of review/commit/Done procedure\n* target: pointer-only prompt; ≥1 clean full-cycle drain\n* measured over: first 2 cycles after the re-scope lands\n* how we'll know: prompt template diff + `drain-cycle grade` over `~/.drain-cycle/runs/*.json`\n\n*Layer 1: correctness · Layer 2: golden-path test*\n\n**KR2 (commit)** — every worker exit in the window produces a run-log entry with exit state, verify/prep verdicts, and halt_reason where applicable; zero uninspectable drains *(foundation)*\n\n* baseline: run logs exist; verify-flow verdicts not recorded in them\n* target: 100% of worker exits logged including verdicts\n* measured over: first 2 cycles after the re-scope lands\n* how we'll know: schema check one-liner over `~/.drain-cycle/runs/*.json` (every entry has exit state; halted entries have halt_reason; verify-flow entries have a verdict field)\n\n*Layer 1: discipline · Layer 2: artefact-exists + sections-complete*\n\n**KR3 (commit)** — the supervisor carries zero workflow prose: worker prompt template ≤15 lines, no skill-procedure steps, test suite green *(brake)*\n\n* baseline: \\~30-line procedure tails in `drain_cycle/prompt.py`\n* target: ≤15-line pointer prompt; `pytest` exits 0\n* measured over: by cycle close\n* how we'll know: `wc -l` on the prompt template + grep for procedure verbs (review/fix/commit/push sequences) + pytest exit code\n\n*Layer 1: maintenance · Layer 2: structural cap*\n\n*Dimensions: correctness / discipline / maintenance — all distinct per rule 5b.*\n\n**Affected repos:** drain-cycle, agent-skills-shaper (entry-skill contract)\n\n**Appetite:** \\~7 issues\n\n**Kill condition:** If pointer-only prompts cause 3 consecutive halted drains that the inlined prompt would have completed, stop — restore the inlined tail and keep the re-scope to guardrails and logging only.\n\n**Project type:** 2 — personal product (single-user)\n\n**Dominant model tier:** Balanced — bounded Python refactors with an existing test suite; the prompt/skill contract design is the Frontier exception.\n\n---\n\n*Shaped 2026-06-10 via /shape:initiative. Depends on the execution initiative (\"Issues drain end-to-end on first-party skills\") — the entry skill must exist before the prompt can point at it. Review package:* `agent-skills-shaper/docs/ideas/lifecycle-expansion.md`*. {"id":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","icon":null,"color":"#bec2c8","name":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","summary":"Layer-1 enforcement/recording/grading in drain-cycle: verifier-gated Done + run-log verdicts + grading, around the pack's already-shipped exec:* skills. So hard tickets don't silently land with missing AC.","description":"# Record and gate the Outcome Verifier verdict before a hard ticket lands\n\ndrain-cycle now supervises a pack of `exec:*` skills: the worker drives a Linear issue from pickup to merged PR, and the supervisor advances on the artifacts it leaves behind. One artifact it does not yet read is the proof the work is done. Under design decision §1 the worker moves its own issue to Done; the supervisor observes only process exit, never task success — so a hard ticket can land with its acceptance criteria unmet, and nothing records whether the outcome was checked.\n\nThe `exec:*` skills already produce that proof — `exec:verify` emits an Outcome Verifier verdict, `exec:pr-prepare` a PR-routing decision — but the verdicts evaporate at the layer boundary: the supervisor has nowhere to record them and no contract that reads them.\n\nThis project keeps drain-cycle's Layer-1 work only: record those verdicts, gate Done on the outcome verdict, and grade the result. For the operator draining outcome-focused tickets, hard tickets stop shipping with missing AC, and every drained ticket's outcome becomes inspectable after the fact.\n\n## Goal\n\nToday a verify-flow ticket reaches Done on the worker's own say-so (§1), with no recorded check. This project drives that to **0 verify-flow Dones without a recorded** `outcome_verdict`, and **≥ 3 of the next 5 hard tickets shipping and merging with no operator-pushed fix commits**.\n\n## What this project covers, and what it does not\n\nIn scope — drain-cycle's Layer 1 only:\n\n* The run-log schema fields the verdicts land in: `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]`, `flow`, plus matching OTel span attributes.\n* The `verify` Linear label that selects the flow, resolved alongside `repo:` and `model:`.\n* The verifier-gated Done contract, which supersedes design decision §1 (the agent self-asserts Done).\n* Grading: `drain-cycle grade-draft <issue>` and `drain-cycle grade --flow=verify`.\n\nOut of scope — delivered elsewhere:\n\n* Authoring the `exec:*` skills — owned by \"Issues drain end-to-end on first-party skills\" (done).\n* The thin-supervisor prompt re-scope and the `exec:*` cutover — owned by \"drain-cycle supervises; the pack owns the workflow\".\n\n## Key results\n\n**KR1 — ship hard tickets without operator rescue commits** *(stretch · bet)*\n\n* Baseline: the recording and enforcement layer doesn't exist, so Done can be self-asserted unverified.\n* Target: ≥ 3 of 5 hard tickets ship and merge with no operator-pushed fix commits.\n* Measured over: the first 5 hard tickets drained with the verify flow (~2 cycles).\n* How we'll know: `drain-cycle grade-draft` auto-drafts a per-ticket grade at `~/.drain-cycle/grades/<issue>.md` for the operator to confirm; `drain-cycle grade --flow=verify` reports the rolled-up pass-rate.\n\n**KR2 — every verify-flow Done carries a recorded verdict** *(commit · brake)*\n\n* Baseline: silent self-assertion is possible today (§1); the past count is unknown because the recording fields don't exist yet.\n* Target: 0 tickets reach Done under the verify flow without an `outcome_verdict` in the run log.\n* Measured over: every cycle that runs the verify flow, capped at 10 cycles.\n* How we'll know: `drain-cycle grade` fails any entry where `flow == \"verify\"` **and** `final_linear_state == \"completed\"` **and** `outcome_verdict` is absent — exit code ≠ 0 on any violation.\n\n**KR3 — every verify-flow PR is review-ready or correctly auto-merged** *(commit · brake)*\n\n* Baseline: PRs from drained tickets carry whatever body the worker produced — typically a one-line subject and no review guidance.\n* Target: 0 PRs submitted under the verify flow without either (a) a structured What / Why / Focus body or (b) an auto-merge label with reasoning.\n* Measured over: every PR submitted under the verify flow, for the project's duration.\n* How we'll know: the prep step writes its decision to `prep_verdict`; `drain-cycle grade` fails any verify-flow entry where `prep_verdict` is absent or claims a structured body the PR doesn't have.\n\n## Kill condition\n\nAfter the verify flow runs on 5+ hard tickets across 2 cycles, kill the contract if the Outcome Verifier produces zero true-positive findings — every halt a false positive, and verifier-passed Dones no better in PR review than default-flow drains. On kill: revert enforcement (milestone 3), keep the recording fields (cheap and useful), and record the negative result in `docs/design-decisions.md`.\n\n## Scope and context\n\n* **Design context:** `docs/architecture.md` (the two-layer split and the verdict-handoff seam) and `docs/design-decisions.md` §1 (the verifier-gated-Done supersession). This description is the source of truth for scope, KRs, and the kill condition.","url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/project/multi-agent-collaboration-for-correctness-5a36114c586c","createdAt":"2026-05-28T10:17:39.942Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:22:24.952Z","startedAt":"2026-06-01T06:52:26.789Z","completedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"startDate":"2026-06-01","startDateResolution":null,"targetDate":null,"targetDateResolution":null,"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"labels":[],"initiatives":[],"lead":{"id":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","name":"Anton Babushkin"},"status":{"id":"2941e5ab-173a-4b38-8032-c21209bb8883","name":"Backlog","type":"backlog"},"teams":[{"id":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","name":"Personal","key":"ABA"}]}

> AGENT

Short answer: they're **not duplicates, but they collide on one load-bearing seam** — and that collision is a real reason not to keep running them as two parallel backlog projects. Here's the picture.

## What each one actually owns

**"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness"** (ABA, 16 issues) — the *outcome-correctness* bet: record the `exec:*` verdicts (`outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]`) in the run log, **gate Done** on the outcome verdict, and **grade** the result. KRs are about hard tickets not landing with missing AC.

**"drain-cycle supervises; the pack owns the workflow"** (5 issues, N01–N05) — the *supervisor-thinness* bet: shrink the worker prompt to a pointer, move workflow prose into the pack, vendor-agnostic. "Third of three" in the lifecycle expansion, own milestones D1–D3.

Those are genuinely separable goals. You could gate-and-grade verdicts without thinning the prompt, and vice versa. So a wholesale merge would just make one fat project.

## But they overlap on exactly the dangerous part

Both projects write the **same run-log verdict fields**, from **two independently-decided contracts**:

- Correctness project: `ABA-321` (run-log schema: `outcome_verdict`/`prep_verdict`/`responder_runs`), `ABA-330` (wire `prep_verdict`), `ABA-328` (halt-on-fail), `ABA-334` (`grade --flow=verify`).
- Supervisor project: `ABA-373` (N04 — supervisor records verdicts on every exit), `ABA-372` (N03 — pack skills emit verdicts into the handoff), and `ABA-370` (N01 design doc that **"extends the inter-skill handoff contract to the supervisor seam"**).

The run-log/handoff schema is a one-way door. `ABA-321` assumes the *old* schema; `ABA-370`/`373` re-decide it two weeks later under a cleaner v2 handoff contract. Leaving both alive means deciding the same schema twice and risking contradictory issues.

## Why the correctness project is the stale one

It was shaped **2026-05-28**, before the two-layer architecture split and the `exec:*` cutover. Evidence it's drifted:
- Its own description carves out skill-authoring to "Issues drain end-to-end on first-party skills" — **already Done**.
- Half its issues are already **Done or Canceled** (ABA-337/327/333/323/322 done; 326/336/335/332 canceled).
- Its live remainder (`321/330/328/334/393/320`) *is* the "record + gate + grade verdicts" work that the supervisor project's N03/N04 now re-describe against the v2 contract.

## Recommendation

Don't merge everything, and don't leave both running. Split cleanly on the seam, with **one owner for the run-log schema**:

- Keep the **correctness project** as the single owner of *record → gate → grade verdicts* (rebase its live issues onto the N01 v2 handoff schema; close the stale-assumption ones).
- Strip the **supervisor project** down to what's uniquely its: prompt-thinning (KR1 + KR3). Its KR2/N03/N04 become a pointer at the correctness project rather than a second schema decision.

That removes the collision without collapsing two real bets into one.

Which way do you want to take it?

{"id":"ABA-370","title":"N01 — Thin-supervisor contract design doc (before build)","description":"`design-doc` · serves **KR3** · **Rule A1 branch** (D1 trips one trigger: one-way-door contract on shared infrastructure across two repos) · blocks N02–N04 · blocked by A/N04 (<issue id=\"6440b109-6e23-43b2-b176-38cc144bb14c\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-361/n04-execution-workflow-design-doc-before-build\">ABA-361</issue>) — this doc *extends* A's inter-skill handoff contract to the supervisor seam; it does not re-decide the skill graph. The initiative's flagged Frontier exception.\n\n**Delegates to:** design-doc skill (Rule A1 design doc — produced before the build nodes are picked up).\n**On pickup — before any build node in this plan:** produce the design doc via the `design-doc` skill and accept it through the `plan-review` exit gate. N02–N04's task breakdown waits until this is accepted.\n\n## What\n\nThe design doc for the supervisor↔worker contract: which segments the ≤15-line prompt template carries (issue context, worktree, base branch, skill pointer — whether the resume directive counts as process context or moves, and **how the per-issue stack/push policy reaches the worker**: `stack` is a supervisor-resolved fact (`--no-stack`, `push_to_main_repos`, `orchestrator.py:505`) that today selects the preamble variant, so the pointer-only template must still transmit it as a process segment); the `.drain-handoff.json` schema v2 — the verdict fields (`outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `flow`, halt reason on non-Done exits), who writes each and who reads each; the boundary chart allocating every current prompt/orchestrator concern to *process* (supervisor keeps: spawn, guardrails, halt, resume, grade) or *workflow* (pack owns: everything else, including verify-flow routing); and the vendor-agnostic prose constraints that let a non-Claude worker follow the same pointer.\n\n## Why\n\nOne accepted contract lets the prompt re-scope and both verdict halves build against a stable seam instead of three locally-sensible interfaces negotiated through PRs. Rejected alternative: re-scope the prompt ad hoc and let the handoff schema grow fields as needs appear — schema drift across two repos with no single source, exactly the two-sources-of-truth failure the thin-supervisor architecture exists to end. Unblocks N02–N04; gives any future non-Claude worker a written contract to be tested against.\n\n## Completion\n\nAn accepted design doc covering: the prompt-segment allocation with the ≤15-line budget spent line by line — including the per-issue stack/push-policy signal and the grep pattern that defines \"procedure verb\" for KR3's check; the handoff schema v2 with writer/reader allocation per field; the process-vs-workflow boundary chart (including where `flow.py`'s verify-label routing and `prompt.py`'s shape-task directive go); and the vendor-agnostic constraints. Accepted through the `plan-review` gate.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Initiative A's design doc (A/N04, <issue id=\"6440b109-6e23-43b2-b176-38cc144bb14c\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-361/n04-execution-workflow-design-doc-before-build\">ABA-361</issue>) is accepted before this one is drafted — this doc extends its handoff contract rather than competing with it *(to-verify — if it slips, this node inherits its namespace and contract questions and grows accordingly)*\n* The ≤15-line cap is measured on the worker prompt template, with process segments counted inside the budget *(verified — KR3's own measurement clause:* `wc -l` *on the template plus grep for procedure verbs)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** this contract drifts from A/N04's inter-skill handoff contract — two documents describing one file's schema. *Mitigation:* schema v2 amends A/N04's handoff table in place (one table, both initiatives cite it); this doc adds the supervisor-seam columns rather than copying the table.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] Frame the constraints and draft ≥2 alternatives for the prompt-segment allocation and handoff schema v2 · Done when: the doc presents the alternatives with trade-offs against the ≤15-line cap and vendor portability · Model: Frontier · risk one-way · review elevated · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = H·H·L·H·L · companions code-review-and-quality\n- [ ] Record the decision (boundary chart + schema v2 + segment allocation) and pass the plan-review exit gate · Done when: the design doc is accepted and N02–N04 can break tasks against it · Model: Frontier · risk one-way · review elevated · axes H·H·L·H·L · companions code-review-and-quality\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N01](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D1-supervisor-sheds-workflow-prose/N01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc.md>)","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-370/n01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc-before-build","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-370-n01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc-before-build","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:07.387Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:26:16.684Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["high","opus","agent-skills-shaper"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-371","title":"N02 — Pointer-only prompt template","description":"`story` · serves **KR3** · **skeleton** (the initiative's walking skeleton — the thinnest supervisor that still drains an issue end-to-end) · lands in the **drain-cycle** repo · blocked by N01 (task breakdown waits for the accepted contract), A/N05 (<issue id=\"4c68570a-7b0f-4e76-b950-4bd705f26046\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-363/n05-entry-skill-the-front-door\">ABA-363</issue> — an entry skill must exist) and A/N10 (<issue id=\"90eb9224-9068-4b91-bd61-73d35697611f\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-367/n10-drain-cycle-prompt-pointer\">ABA-367</issue> — the pointer swap lands first; this node deletes the machinery around the pointer, so the branches must chain in that order).\n\n**Delegates to:** at-pickup task breakdown (story). The diff is subtractive — collapse, don't rewrite.\n\n## What\n\nAs the drain-cycle supervisor, I want my worker prompt reduced to issue context plus process segments plus a single skill pointer, so that the intra-issue workflow lives only in the pack and any vendor's worker can follow it unchanged. Concretely: `_normal_preamble`, `_stack_preamble`, `_TAIL`, and `_STACK_TAIL` in `drain_cycle/prompt.py` collapse into one ≤15-line template, and workflow routing the supervisor still carries (the shape-task directive; `flow.py`'s verify-label branch) moves or dies per N01's boundary chart.\n\n## Why\n\nSubtraction is the whole re-scope — KR3 is a brake measured by `wc -l`, grep, and pytest, and every line that leaves the prompt is a line that can never again drift from the pack's prose. Rejected alternative: keep the inlined procedure behind a fallback flag next to the pointer — two sources of truth, and workers follow the stale one (the same rejection A/N10 recorded for its scope). Unblocks N05's validation cycles and opens the non-Claude-worker door.\n\n## Completion\n\n* **Done when:** `wc -l` on the emitted worker prompt template is ≤15 and grep for procedure-verb sequences (review/fix/commit/push) over `drain_cycle/` returns empty.\n* **Done when:** pytest exits 0 with the test suite updated to the new template.\n* **Done when:** one smoke drain on a low-stakes issue reaches PR through the new template with no inlined-procedure fallback observed in the transcript.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* A/N10's pointer swap has landed before pickup — this node edits the same file and assumes the third-party reference is already gone *(to-verify — checked at pickup via the blockedBy edge; if N10 slipped, this node halts rather than absorbing its scope)*\n* The pack's PR-finishing skill (A/N08) carries the handoff-write and review-trail steps, so the prompt can drop them *(to-verify — checked against the accepted A/N04 contract at pickup; a gap re-opens N01's boundary chart, not this node's scope)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** the ≤15-line budget proves too tight for genuine process context (resume directive, stack signal, worktree facts) and the cap gets quietly exceeded. *Mitigation:* N01 allocates the budget line by line before this node breaks tasks; if a process segment doesn't fit, the KR cap is challenged at plan-review — never silently blown.\n* **Risk:** dropping the inlined procedure degrades worker behaviour mid-cycle. *Mitigation:* the kill condition is the brake — 3 consecutive halted drains the inlined prompt would have completed restores the inlined tail; the collapse is one commit, a two-way door.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Collapse the preamble/tail variants in `prompt.py` into the ≤15-line pointer template per the accepted contract (test updates folded in) · Done when: `wc -l` on the emitted template is ≤15 and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·L·L·M·L\n- [ ] Remove or relocate the supervisor's workflow routing (shape-task directive, verify-label branch) per N01's boundary chart · Done when: grep for procedure-verb sequences over `drain_cycle/` returns empty and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes M·L·L·M·L\n- [ ] Smoke-drain one low-stakes issue through the new template · Done when: the drain reaches PR with no inlined-procedure fallback observed in the transcript · Model: Fast · axes M·M·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N02](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D1-supervisor-sheds-workflow-prose/N02-pointer-only-prompt-template.md>)","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-371/n02-pointer-only-prompt-template","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-371-n02-pointer-only-prompt-template","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:35.577Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:26:17.981Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["skeleton","haiku","drain-cycle"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-372","title":"N03 — Pack skills emit verdicts into the handoff","description":"`story` · serves **KR2** · lands in the **agent-skills-shaper** repo (extends the execution skills initiative A authors) · blocked by N01 (task breakdown waits for the accepted handoff schema v2) and A's D2 skill stack (<issue id=\"4c68570a-7b0f-4e76-b950-4bd705f26046\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-363/n05-entry-skill-the-front-door\">ABA-363</issue>–366 — this node edits those skills' verify and PR-finishing steps, so it cannot start before they exist).\n\n**Delegates to:** at-pickup task breakdown (story). The diff extends A's skills at their handoff seams — it does not add new skills.\n\n## What\n\nAs the operator inspecting a drain, I want the pack's verify and PR-preparation steps to write their verdicts into the handoff file, so that the supervisor can record them without owning any workflow knowledge. The handoff file already carries `pr_title`/`pr_body`/`findings`; this node adds the schema-v2 verdict fields (`outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `flow`) at the workflow steps that produce them, plus the halt reason on blocked/non-Done exits.\n\n## Why\n\nThe handoff file is already the proven worker→supervisor channel, so verdicts ride the existing seam with zero new transport. Rejected alternative: the supervisor parses worker transcripts for verdicts — couples inspection to one vendor's transcript format, which is exactly the coupling this initiative exists to remove. Unblocks N04's read side; the write-half of KR2.\n\n## Completion\n\n* **Done when:** a toy-issue dry run through the execution skills leaves a handoff file with `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, and `flow` populated per schema v2.\n* **Done when:** a blocked-issue dry run leaves a handoff naming the halt reason while the issue stays In Progress per workflow governance.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* A's execution skills have distinct verify and PR-preparation steps where verdict emission can attach *(verified — A/N05's front door names verify and PR as named delegations, and A/N08 owns the handoff write; the seams exist by contract)*\n* Verdict emission is a light extension of those steps, not a redesign *(to-verify — if a step produces no structured verdict object at all, this node adds the object at the skill seam; the supervisor side is unaffected either way)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** the fields the pack writes drift from the fields the supervisor reads — two repos, one schema. *Mitigation:* schema v2 lives in one table (N01's contract, amending A/N04's); N04's tests parse a fixture handoff written verbatim from that table, so drift fails a test before it fails a drain.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Add verdict emission to the verify and PR-finishing steps per schema v2 (fixture handoff example folded in) · Done when: a toy-issue dry run leaves a handoff with outcome_verdict, prep_verdict, and flow populated per schema v2 · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·L·L·L·L\n- [ ] Write the blocked/halt emission path · Done when: a blocked-issue dry run leaves a handoff naming the halt reason and the issue status is unchanged · Model: Fast · axes M·M·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N03](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D2-every-drain-inspectable/N03-pack-skills-emit-verdicts.md>)","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-372/n03-pack-skills-emit-verdicts-into-the-handoff","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-372-n03-pack-skills-emit-verdicts-into-the-handoff","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:48.358Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:26:19.154Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["haiku","agent-skills-shaper"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-373","title":"N04 — Supervisor records verdicts on every exit","description":"`story` · serves **KR2** · lands in the **drain-cycle** repo · blocked by N01 (task breakdown waits for the accepted handoff schema v2). Buildable in parallel with N03: the read side tests against fixture handoffs written from the schema table, not against N03's live output.\n\n**Delegates to:** at-pickup task breakdown (story).\n\n## What\n\nAs the operator grading cycles, I want every worker exit — Done, halted, errored — to land a run-log entry with verdicts and halt_reason, so that no drain is uninspectable. The run-log schema already has the fields; nothing populates them: `outcome_verdict` and `prep_verdict` are initialised to `None` at `orchestrator.py:647-649` and never assigned. This node has `handoff.py` parse the schema-v2 verdict fields and the orchestrator land them on every `append_entry` path.\n\n## Why\n\nReading verdicts from the handoff closes KR2 with a bounded diff on a file the orchestrator already reads at every Done exit — no new I/O, no new failure mode. Rejected alternative: the supervisor re-derives verdicts by inspecting PRs and Linear comments — re-creates workflow knowledge inside the supervisor, violating the boundary this initiative draws. Unblocks N05's grading (the schema check is one of its three measurements).\n\n## Completion\n\n* **Done when:** a stubbed Done-path drain writes a run-log entry with `flow`, `outcome_verdict`, and `prep_verdict` populated from the handoff, pytest green.\n* **Done when:** a forced-halt test writes an entry carrying `halt_reason` plus whatever verdicts the partial handoff held.\n* **Done when:** the KR2 schema check exits 0 over a compliant run log and non-zero when a Done entry lacks verdicts.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Every exit path flows through `runlog.append_entry`, so populating at that seam covers Done, halted, and errored exits alike *(verified — Done and halt paths both call* `log.append_entry` *in [orchestrator.py](<http://orchestrator.py>); the run-log docstring documents the halt-entry contract)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** a worker that crashes before writing the handoff leaves verdicts null, and the schema check reads the drain as a KR2 failure when it is actually inspectable via halt_reason. *Mitigation:* the schema check encodes \"inspectable\", not \"non-null\": null verdicts are compliant when `halt_reason` (or a non-Done exit state) explains them; a null on a Done entry is the violation.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Parse schema-v2 verdict fields in `handoff.py` and populate Done-path run-log entries (fixture-handoff test folded in) · Done when: a stubbed drain writes an entry with flow, outcome_verdict, and prep_verdict populated and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·L·L·L·L\n- [ ] Populate halt/error exits with halt_reason plus any partial-handoff verdicts · Done when: a forced-halt test writes an entry where halt_reason is non-null and available verdicts are carried · Model: Fast · axes M·L·L·L·L\n- [ ] Add the KR2 schema check (one-liner or small script beside the run logs) · Done when: the check exits 0 over a compliant run log and non-zero when a Done entry lacks verdicts · Model: Fast · axes L·L·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N04](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D2-every-drain-inspectable/N04-supervisor-records-verdicts.md>)","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-373/n04-supervisor-records-verdicts-on-every-exit","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-373-n04-supervisor-records-verdicts-on-every-exit","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:59.668Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:26:21.453Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["haiku","drain-cycle"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-374","title":"N05 — Validation: 2 cycles on the pointer prompt","description":"`experiment` · serves **KR1** · **acceptance: verifies D3** — close LAST so the milestone hitting 100% coincides with the KR observation · blocked by N02–N04 (everything under test must have landed) and A/N09 (<issue id=\"bfcdbf63-3a34-40b4-a50d-6aa30fe539e6\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-368/n09-validation-drains-5-issues-45\">ABA-368</issue> — the execution skills should be proven on live drains before the supervisor is thinned, so a failure here indicts the re-scope, not the skills).\n\n**Delegates to:** product-spike (experiment discipline — hypothesis, success metric, written finding).\n**On pickup:** run the experiment and write the finding (confirmed / falsified). Not a build node: honest outcomes are *confirmed* or *falsified*, not *shipped*. Because the measurement window is shared, this node also runs the KR2 schema check over the same logs.\n\n## What\n\nDrain the first 2 real cycles after the re-scope lands with the pointer-only prompt, and grade the bet: did ≥1 cycle complete with zero manual fix commits? Each cycle's evidence is three measurements — `drain-cycle grade` over the run logs, the prompt-template diff (the ≤15-line template in force), and the KR2 schema check (every exit inspectable). Watch the kill condition throughout. Write the finding.\n\n## Why\n\nKR1 is the stretch bet, and only live cycles can grade it — N02's smoke drain proves one issue flows, not that a whole cycle's mess (halts, resumes, stacked PRs, mixed repos) survives a supervisor that no longer says how to work. Rejected alternative: declare victory on the smoke drain — the unverified-claim failure this pack exists to prevent. `acceptance` because D3's Done is irreducible to its children: there is no build node whose completion implies a cycle drains.\n\n## Completion\n\n**Hypothesis:** a full cycle drains to completion with the pointer-only prompt at the execution initiative's quality bar (no manual fix commits), with every worker exit inspectable.\n\n**Success metric:** `drain-cycle grade` + prompt-template diff + KR2 schema check over the first 2 cycles after the re-scope lands = **≥1 clean full-cycle drain**.\n\nZero clean drains in the window **falsifies** the bet — the re-scope is re-shaped, not extended. Three consecutive halted drains that the inlined prompt would have completed fires the initiative **kill condition**: restore the inlined tail; keep the re-scope to guardrails and logging only.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Two cycles of ordinary work are available in the measurement window *(verified — the KR's own \"measured over\" clause anticipates this window, and the backlog convention keeps a cycle stream)*\n* \"Drains the inlined prompt would have completed\" is judgeable from halt transcripts *(to-verify — the kill condition needs a per-halt judgement note; if a halt is ambiguous, it does not count toward the 3)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** the 2 cycles are dominated by markdown-pack work and a clean drain overstates the re-scope's generality. *Mitigation:* include at least one drain-cycle (Python) issue across the window so both repo types exercise the pointer prompt.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Drain the first post-re-scope cycle; record grade, template line count, and schema-check result in a trial note · Done when: the run log and trial note for cycle 1 exist with all three measurements · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·M·L·L·L\n- [ ] `acceptance` — Drain the second cycle; evaluate the ≥1-clean-cycle metric and the kill condition; write the finding (confirmed / falsified) — this finding is D3's Done condition · Done when: the finding is written with per-cycle evidence links · Model: Balanced · axes H·M·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N05](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D3-full-cycle-on-pointer-prompt/N05-validation-cycle-drains.md>)","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-374/n05-validation-2-cycles-on-the-pointer-prompt","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-374-n05-validation-2-cycles-on-the-pointer-prompt","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:49:25.532Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:26:22.860Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["acceptance","product-spike","sonnet","agent-skills-shaper"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

  "description": "> **Merged 2026-06-15 into [Multi-agent collaboration for correctness](https://linear.app/ababushkin/project/multi-agent-collaboration-for-correctness-5a36114c586c).** N01–N05 (ABA-370–374) moved there. This project and the correctness project both wrote the same run-log verdict fields (`outcome_verdict`/`prep_verdict`) from two independently-decided contracts — a one-way-door schema decided twice. Consolidated under the correctness project so the schema has a single owner; N01's handoff schema v2 is the contract the older recording issues (ABA-321/330) rebase onto. Original scope below for reference.\n\n---\n\n**Goal:** For Anton (single user), make unattended cycle drains run with the supervisor owning only process concerns — spawn, guardrails, halt, resume, grade — so any vendor's worker can follow the pack's workflow prose unchanged.\n\n**Key results:**\n\n**KR1 (stretch)** — a full cycle drains to completion with the worker prompt reduced to issue context + a single skill pointer (zero inlined workflow steps), at the execution initiative's quality bar (no manual fix commits) *(bet)*\n\n**KR2 (commit)** — every worker exit in the window produces a run-log entry with exit state, verify/prep verdicts, and halt_reason where applicable; zero uninspectable drains *(foundation)*\n\n**KR3 (commit)** — the supervisor carries zero workflow prose: worker prompt template ≤15 lines, no skill-procedure steps, test suite green *(brake)*\n\n**Affected repos:** drain-cycle, agent-skills-shaper (entry-skill contract)\n\n**Appetite:** ~7 issues\n\n**Kill condition:** If pointer-only prompts cause 3 consecutive halted drains that the inlined prompt would have completed, stop — restore the inlined tail and keep the re-scope to guardrails and logging only.\n\n**Project type:** 2 — personal product (single-user)\n\n---\n\n*Shaped 2026-06-10 via /shape:initiative. {"id":"ABA-370","title":"N01 — Thin-supervisor contract design doc (before build)","description":"`design-doc` · serves **KR3** · **Rule A1 branch** (D1 trips one trigger: one-way-door contract on shared infrastructure across two repos) · blocks N02–N04 · blocked by A/N04 (<issue id=\"6440b109-6e23-43b2-b176-38cc144bb14c\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-361/n04-execution-workflow-design-doc-before-build\">ABA-361</issue>) — this doc *extends* A's inter-skill handoff contract to the supervisor seam; it does not re-decide the skill graph. The initiative's flagged Frontier exception.\n\n**Delegates to:** design-doc skill (Rule A1 design doc — produced before the build nodes are picked up).\n**On pickup — before any build node in this plan:** produce the design doc via the `design-doc` skill and accept it through the `plan-review` exit gate. N02–N04's task breakdown waits until this is accepted.\n\n## What\n\nThe design doc for the supervisor↔worker contract: which segments the ≤15-line prompt template carries (issue context, worktree, base branch, skill pointer — whether the resume directive counts as process context or moves, and **how the per-issue stack/push policy reaches the worker**: `stack` is a supervisor-resolved fact (`--no-stack`, `push_to_main_repos`, `orchestrator.py:505`) that today selects the preamble variant, so the pointer-only template must still transmit it as a process segment); the `.drain-handoff.json` schema v2 — the verdict fields (`outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `flow`, halt reason on non-Done exits), who writes each and who reads each; the boundary chart allocating every current prompt/orchestrator concern to *process* (supervisor keeps: spawn, guardrails, halt, resume, grade) or *workflow* (pack owns: everything else, including verify-flow routing); and the vendor-agnostic prose constraints that let a non-Claude worker follow the same pointer.\n\n## Why\n\nOne accepted contract lets the prompt re-scope and both verdict halves build against a stable seam instead of three locally-sensible interfaces negotiated through PRs. Rejected alternative: re-scope the prompt ad hoc and let the handoff schema grow fields as needs appear — schema drift across two repos with no single source, exactly the two-sources-of-truth failure the thin-supervisor architecture exists to end. Unblocks N02–N04; gives any future non-Claude worker a written contract to be tested against.\n\n## Completion\n\nAn accepted design doc covering: the prompt-segment allocation with the ≤15-line budget spent line by line — including the per-issue stack/push-policy signal and the grep pattern that defines \"procedure verb\" for KR3's check; the handoff schema v2 with writer/reader allocation per field; the process-vs-workflow boundary chart (including where `flow.py`'s verify-label routing and `prompt.py`'s shape-task directive go); and the vendor-agnostic constraints. Accepted through the `plan-review` gate.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Initiative A's design doc (A/N04, <issue id=\"6440b109-6e23-43b2-b176-38cc144bb14c\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-361/n04-execution-workflow-design-doc-before-build\">ABA-361</issue>) is accepted before this one is drafted — this doc extends its handoff contract rather than competing with it *(to-verify — if it slips, this node inherits its namespace and contract questions and grows accordingly)*\n* The ≤15-line cap is measured on the worker prompt template, with process segments counted inside the budget *(verified — KR3's own measurement clause:* `wc -l` *on the template plus grep for procedure verbs)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** this contract drifts from A/N04's inter-skill handoff contract — two documents describing one file's schema. *Mitigation:* schema v2 amends A/N04's handoff table in place (one table, both initiatives cite it); this doc adds the supervisor-seam columns rather than copying the table.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] Frame the constraints and draft ≥2 alternatives for the prompt-segment allocation and handoff schema v2 · Done when: the doc presents the alternatives with trade-offs against the ≤15-line cap and vendor portability · Model: Frontier · risk one-way · review elevated · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = H·H·L·H·L · companions code-review-and-quality\n- [ ] Record the decision (boundary chart + schema v2 + segment allocation) and pass the plan-review exit gate · Done when: the design doc is accepted and N02–N04 can break tasks against it · Model: Frontier · risk one-way · review elevated · axes H·H·L·H·L · companions code-review-and-quality\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N01](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D1-supervisor-sheds-workflow-prose/N01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc.md>)","projectMilestone":{"id":"7795d606-90a7-443c-b390-d322ef8dea3a","name":"5 — Thin the supervisor (pointer-only prompt)"},"priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-370/n01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc-before-build","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-370-n01-thin-supervisor-contract-design-doc-before-build","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:07.387Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:28:20.446Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["high","opus","agent-skills-shaper"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-371","title":"N02 — Pointer-only prompt template","description":"`story` · serves **KR3** · **skeleton** (the initiative's walking skeleton — the thinnest supervisor that still drains an issue end-to-end) · lands in the **drain-cycle** repo · blocked by N01 (task breakdown waits for the accepted contract), A/N05 (<issue id=\"4c68570a-7b0f-4e76-b950-4bd705f26046\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-363/n05-entry-skill-the-front-door\">ABA-363</issue> — an entry skill must exist) and A/N10 (<issue id=\"90eb9224-9068-4b91-bd61-73d35697611f\" href=\"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-367/n10-drain-cycle-prompt-pointer\">ABA-367</issue> — the pointer swap lands first; this node deletes the machinery around the pointer, so the branches must chain in that order).\n\n**Delegates to:** at-pickup task breakdown (story). The diff is subtractive — collapse, don't rewrite.\n\n## What\n\nAs the drain-cycle supervisor, I want my worker prompt reduced to issue context plus process segments plus a single skill pointer, so that the intra-issue workflow lives only in the pack and any vendor's worker can follow it unchanged. Concretely: `_normal_preamble`, `_stack_preamble`, `_TAIL`, and `_STACK_TAIL` in `drain_cycle/prompt.py` collapse into one ≤15-line template, and workflow routing the supervisor still carries (the shape-task directive; `flow.py`'s verify-label branch) moves or dies per N01's boundary chart.\n\n## Why\n\nSubtraction is the whole re-scope — KR3 is a brake measured by `wc -l`, grep, and pytest, and every line that leaves the prompt is a line that can never again drift from the pack's prose. Rejected alternative: keep the inlined procedure behind a fallback flag next to the pointer — two sources of truth, and workers follow the stale one (the same rejection A/N10 recorded for its scope). Unblocks N05's validation cycles and opens the non-Claude-worker door.\n\n## Completion\n\n* **Done when:** `wc -l` on the emitted worker prompt template is ≤15 and grep for procedure-verb sequences (review/fix/commit/push) over `drain_cycle/` returns empty.\n* **Done when:** pytest exits 0 with the test suite updated to the new template.\n* **Done when:** one smoke drain on a low-stakes issue reaches PR through the new template with no inlined-procedure fallback observed in the transcript.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* A/N10's pointer swap has landed before pickup — this node edits the same file and assumes the third-party reference is already gone *(to-verify — checked at pickup via the blockedBy edge; if N10 slipped, this node halts rather than absorbing its scope)*\n* The pack's PR-finishing skill (A/N08) carries the handoff-write and review-trail steps, so the prompt can drop them *(to-verify — checked against the accepted A/N04 contract at pickup; a gap re-opens N01's boundary chart, not this node's scope)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** the ≤15-line budget proves too tight for genuine process context (resume directive, stack signal, worktree facts) and the cap gets quietly exceeded. *Mitigation:* N01 allocates the budget line by line before this node breaks tasks; if a process segment doesn't fit, the KR cap is challenged at plan-review — never silently blown.\n* **Risk:** dropping the inlined procedure degrades worker behaviour mid-cycle. *Mitigation:* the kill condition is the brake — 3 consecutive halted drains the inlined prompt would have completed restores the inlined tail; the collapse is one commit, a two-way door.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Collapse the preamble/tail variants in `prompt.py` into the ≤15-line pointer template per the accepted contract (test updates folded in) · Done when: `wc -l` on the emitted template is ≤15 and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·L·L·M·L\n- [ ] Remove or relocate the supervisor's workflow routing (shape-task directive, verify-label branch) per N01's boundary chart · Done when: grep for procedure-verb sequences over `drain_cycle/` returns empty and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes M·L·L·M·L\n- [ ] Smoke-drain one low-stakes issue through the new template · Done when: the drain reaches PR with no inlined-procedure fallback observed in the transcript · Model: Fast · axes M·M·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N02](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D1-supervisor-sheds-workflow-prose/N02-pointer-only-prompt-template.md>)","projectMilestone":{"id":"7795d606-90a7-443c-b390-d322ef8dea3a","name":"5 — Thin the supervisor (pointer-only prompt)"},"priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-371/n02-pointer-only-prompt-template","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-371-n02-pointer-only-prompt-template","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:35.577Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:28:21.675Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["skeleton","haiku","drain-cycle"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

The handoff file already carries `pr_title`/`pr_body`/`findings`; this node adds the schema-v2 verdict fields (`outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `flow`) at the workflow steps that produce them, plus the halt reason on blocked/non-Done exits.\n\n## Why\n\nThe handoff file is already the proven worker→supervisor channel, so verdicts ride the existing seam with zero new transport. {"id":"ABA-373","title":"N04 — Supervisor records verdicts on every exit","description":"`story` · serves **KR2** · lands in the **drain-cycle** repo · blocked by N01 (task breakdown waits for the accepted handoff schema v2). Buildable in parallel with N03: the read side tests against fixture handoffs written from the schema table, not against N03's live output.\n\n**Delegates to:** at-pickup task breakdown (story).\n\n## What\n\nAs the operator grading cycles, I want every worker exit — Done, halted, errored — to land a run-log entry with verdicts and halt_reason, so that no drain is uninspectable. The run-log schema already has the fields; nothing populates them: `outcome_verdict` and `prep_verdict` are initialised to `None` at `orchestrator.py:647-649` and never assigned. This node has `handoff.py` parse the schema-v2 verdict fields and the orchestrator land them on every `append_entry` path.\n\n## Why\n\nReading verdicts from the handoff closes KR2 with a bounded diff on a file the orchestrator already reads at every Done exit — no new I/O, no new failure mode. Rejected alternative: the supervisor re-derives verdicts by inspecting PRs and Linear comments — re-creates workflow knowledge inside the supervisor, violating the boundary this initiative draws. Unblocks N05's grading (the schema check is one of its three measurements).\n\n## Completion\n\n* **Done when:** a stubbed Done-path drain writes a run-log entry with `flow`, `outcome_verdict`, and `prep_verdict` populated from the handoff, pytest green.\n* **Done when:** a forced-halt test writes an entry carrying `halt_reason` plus whatever verdicts the partial handoff held.\n* **Done when:** the KR2 schema check exits 0 over a compliant run log and non-zero when a Done entry lacks verdicts.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Every exit path flows through `runlog.append_entry`, so populating at that seam covers Done, halted, and errored exits alike *(verified — Done and halt paths both call* `log.append_entry` *in [orchestrator.py](<http://orchestrator.py>); the run-log docstring documents the halt-entry contract)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** a worker that crashes before writing the handoff leaves verdicts null, and the schema check reads the drain as a KR2 failure when it is actually inspectable via halt_reason. *Mitigation:* the schema check encodes \"inspectable\", not \"non-null\": null verdicts are compliant when `halt_reason` (or a non-Done exit state) explains them; a null on a Done entry is the violation.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Parse schema-v2 verdict fields in `handoff.py` and populate Done-path run-log entries (fixture-handoff test folded in) · Done when: a stubbed drain writes an entry with flow, outcome_verdict, and prep_verdict populated and pytest is green · Model: Fast · axes RC·SC·HS·SR·OR = M·L·L·L·L\n- [ ] Populate halt/error exits with halt_reason plus any partial-handoff verdicts · Done when: a forced-halt test writes an entry where halt_reason is non-null and available verdicts are carried · Model: Fast · axes M·L·L·L·L\n- [ ] Add the KR2 schema check (one-liner or small script beside the run logs) · Done when: the check exits 0 over a compliant run log and non-zero when a Done entry lacks verdicts · Model: Fast · axes L·L·L·L·L\n\n---\n\nPlan source: [N04](<https://github.com/ababushkin/agent-skills-shaper/blob/main/examples/delivery-plans/drain-cycle-thin-supervisor/D2-every-drain-inspectable/N04-supervisor-records-verdicts.md>)","projectMilestone":{"id":"a668c8fa-f026-4247-b3c5-c491c9865642","name":"2 — Record the pack's verdicts in the run log"},"priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-373/n04-supervisor-records-verdicts-on-every-exit","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-373-n04-supervisor-records-verdicts-on-every-exit","createdAt":"2026-06-10T12:48:59.668Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:28:24.028Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["haiku","drain-cycle"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

Because the measurement window is shared, this node also runs the KR2 schema check over the same logs.\n\n## What\n\nDrain the first 2 real cycles after the re-scope lands with the pointer-only prompt, and grade the bet: did ≥1 cycle complete with zero manual fix commits? Write the finding.\n\n## Why\n\nKR1 is the stretch bet, and only live cycles can grade it — N02's smoke drain proves one issue flows, not that a whole cycle's mess (halts, resumes, stacked PRs, mixed repos) survives a supervisor that no longer says how to work. Rejected alternative: declare victory on the smoke drain — the unverified-claim failure this pack exists to prevent. `acceptance` because D3's Done is irreducible to its children: there is no build node whose completion implies a cycle drains.\n\n## Completion\n\n**Hypothesis:** a full cycle drains to completion with the pointer-only prompt at the execution initiative's quality bar (no manual fix commits), with every worker exit inspectable.\n\n**Success metric:** `drain-cycle grade` + prompt-template diff + KR2 schema check over the first 2 cycles after the re-scope lands = **≥1 clean full-cycle drain**.\n\nZero clean drains in the window **falsifies** the bet — the re-scope is re-shaped, not extended. Three consecutive halted drains that the inlined prompt would have completed fires the initiative **kill condition**: restore the inlined tail; keep the re-scope to guardrails and logging only.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Two cycles of ordinary work are available in the measurement window *(verified — the KR's own \"measured over\" clause anticipates this window, and the backlog convention keeps a cycle stream)*\n* \"Drains the inlined prompt would have completed\" is judgeable from halt transcripts *(to-verify — the kill condition needs a per-halt judgement note; if a halt is ambiguous, it does not count toward the 3)*\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* **Risk:** the 2 cycles are dominated by markdown-pack work and a clean drain overstates the re-scope's generality. *Mitigation:* include at least one drain-cycle (Python) issue across the window so both repo types exercise the pointer prompt.\n\n## Tasks\n\n- [ ] `skeleton` — Drain the first post-re-scope cycle; {"id":"ABA-321","title":"Run-log schema: `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]` present (null) on every drain","description":"## What\n\nThree new fields are present on every run-log entry: `outcome_verdict` (null), `prep_verdict` (null), `responder_runs` (empty array). Their shapes are documented in-code.\n\n## Why\n\nThese are the write targets for the Outcome Verifier (M2), PR Preparer (M3), and PR Responder (M4). They must exist before any role can write to them, and must be present on all drains so the grader can inspect them uniformly.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* `outcome_verdict` shape: `{result, findings[], invoked_at}` or `null`.\n* `prep_verdict` shape: `{result, route, reasoning}` or `null`.\n* `responder_runs` is an array of `{comment_ids[], invoked_at, result}` objects, empty by default.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* Introducing new fields on existing schema may break deserialisation in run-log readers if they use strict parsing; a forward-compatible parse strategy must be confirmed.\n* The field shapes must be stable before M2/M3/M4 consume them — any shape change after those milestones begin is expensive.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n* Every drain (verify or default) produces a run-log entry that includes `outcome_verdict: null`, `prep_verdict: null`, `responder_runs: []`.\n* The field shapes match the spec in `docs/initiative-multi-agent-collaboration.md`.\n* Existing run-log readers produce no errors on entries with the new fields.","projectMilestone":{"id":"a668c8fa-f026-4247-b3c5-c491c9865642","name":"2 — Record the pack's verdicts in the run log"},"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-321/run-log-schema-outcome-verdict-prep-verdict-responder-runs-present","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-321-run-log-schema-outcome_verdict-prep_verdict-responder_runs","createdAt":"2026-05-28T11:54:13.803Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T02:33:43.945Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Todo","statusType":"unstarted","labels":["drain-cycle","sonnet"],"attachments":[{"id":"732df775-a520-4f4f-b835-aeb0ca691adc","title":"feat: add outcome_verdict, prep_verdict, responder_runs to run-log schema","subtitle":null,"url":"https://github.com/ababushkin/drain-cycle/pull/8"}],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","assignee":"Anton Babushkin","assigneeId":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1"}

{"id":"ABA-330","title":"Worker invokes PR Preparer after Outcome Verifier pass; `prep_verdict` written to run log","description":"## What\n\nThe drain-cycle worker session invokes `/shape:pr-prepare` after the Outcome Verifier returns pass and before the PR is submitted; `prep_verdict` is written to the run-log entry.\n\n## Why\n\nWithout this wiring, the PR Preparer skill exists but is never invoked — KR3 (every PR has a structured body or auto-merge label) cannot be satisfied mechanically.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* The drain-cycle worker has a defined PR submission step that can be preceded by the PR Preparer invocation.\n* The Graphite stack has already been created by the worker before PR Prepare runs.\n* If PR Preparer crashes or produces no verdict, the drain halts rather than submitting a bare PR.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* The ordering constraint (verifier pass → PR prep → PR submit) must be enforced; a race or skip would produce PRs without the structured body, violating KR3.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n* A `verify`-flow drain invokes `/shape:pr-prepare` after a pass verdict and before PR submission.\n* `prep_verdict` is present and non-null in the run-log entry for every `verify`-flow PR submission.\n* A non-verify-flow drain does not invoke the PR Preparer.\n* `drain-cycle grade` fails any entry where `flow == \"verify\"` AND `final_linear_state == \"completed\"` AND `prep_verdict` is absent.","projectMilestone":{"id":"a668c8fa-f026-4247-b3c5-c491c9865642","name":"2 — Record the pack's verdicts in the run log"},"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-330/worker-invokes-pr-preparer-after-outcome-verifier-pass-prep-verdict","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-330-worker-invokes-pr-preparer-after-outcome-verifier-pass","createdAt":"2026-05-28T11:56:02.084Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:20:43.398Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Todo","statusType":"unstarted","labels":["drain-cycle","sonnet"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","cycleId":"9e17380f-9527-40ef-8523-4e4358f12809"}

{"id":"ABA-321","title":"Run-log schema: `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]` present (null) on every drain","description":"## What\n\nThree new fields are present on every run-log entry: `outcome_verdict` (null), `prep_verdict` (null), `responder_runs` (empty array). Their shapes are documented in-code.\n\n## Why\n\nThese are the write targets for the Outcome Verifier (M2), PR Preparer (M3), and PR Responder (M4). They must exist before any role can write to them, and must be present on all drains so the grader can inspect them uniformly.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* `outcome_verdict` shape: `{result, findings[], invoked_at}` or `null`.\n* `prep_verdict` shape: `{result, route, reasoning}` or `null`.\n* `responder_runs` is an array of `{comment_ids[], invoked_at, result}` objects, empty by default.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* Introducing new fields on existing schema may break deserialisation in run-log readers if they use strict parsing; {"id":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","icon":null,"color":"#bec2c8","name":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","summary":"Layer-1 enforcement/recording/grading in drain-cycle: verifier-gated Done + run-log verdicts + grading, around the pack's already-shipped exec:* skills. So hard tickets don't silently land with missing AC.","description":"# Record and gate the Outcome Verifier verdict before a hard ticket lands\n\ndrain-cycle now supervises a pack of `exec:*` skills: the worker drives a Linear issue from pickup to merged PR, and the supervisor advances on the artifacts it leaves behind. One artifact it does not yet read is the proof the work is done. Under design decision §1 the worker moves its own issue to Done; the supervisor observes only process exit, never task success — so a hard ticket can land with its acceptance criteria unmet, and nothing records whether the outcome was checked.\n\nThe `exec:*` skills already produce that proof — `exec:verify` emits an Outcome Verifier verdict, `exec:pr-prepare` a PR-routing decision — but the verdicts evaporate at the layer boundary: the supervisor has nowhere to record them and no contract that reads them.\n\nThis project keeps drain-cycle's Layer-1 work only: record those verdicts, gate Done on the outcome verdict, and grade the result. For the operator draining outcome-focused tickets, hard tickets stop shipping with missing AC, and every drained ticket's outcome becomes inspectable after the fact.\n\n## Goal\n\nToday a verify-flow ticket reaches Done on the worker's own say-so (§1), with no recorded check. This project drives that to **0 verify-flow Dones without a recorded** `outcome_verdict`, and **≥ 3 of the next 5 hard tickets shipping and merging with no operator-pushed fix commits**.\n\n## What this project covers, and what it does not\n\nIn scope — drain-cycle's Layer 1 only:\n\n* The run-log schema fields the verdicts land in: `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]`, `flow`, plus matching OTel span attributes.\n* The `verify` Linear label that selects the flow, resolved alongside `repo:` and `model:`.\n* The verifier-gated Done contract, which supersedes design decision §1 (the agent self-asserts Done).\n* Grading: `drain-cycle grade-draft <issue>` and `drain-cycle grade --flow=verify`.\n\nOut of scope — delivered elsewhere:\n\n* Authoring the `exec:*` skills — owned by \"Issues drain end-to-end on first-party skills\" (done).\n* The thin-supervisor prompt re-scope and the `exec:*` cutover — owned by \"drain-cycle supervises; the pack owns the workflow\".\n\n## Key results\n\n**KR1 — ship hard tickets without operator rescue commits** *(bet)*\n\n* Baseline: the recording and enforcement layer doesn't exist, so Done can be self-asserted unverified.\n* Target: ≥ 3 of 5 hard tickets ship and merge with no operator-pushed fix commits.\n* Measured over: the first 5 hard tickets drained with the verify flow (~2 cycles).\n* How we'll know: `drain-cycle grade-draft` auto-drafts a per-ticket grade at `~/.drain-cycle/grades/<issue>.md` for the operator to confirm; `drain-cycle grade --flow=verify` reports the rolled-up pass-rate.\n\n**KR2 — every verify-flow Done carries a recorded verdict**\n\n* Baseline: silent self-assertion is possible today (§1); the past count is unknown because the recording fields don't exist yet.\n* Target: 0 tickets reach Done under the verify flow without an `outcome_verdict` in the run log.\n* Measured over: every cycle that runs the verify flow, capped at 10 cycles.\n* How we'll know: `drain-cycle grade` fails any entry where `flow == \"verify\"` **and** `final_linear_state == \"completed\"` **and** `outcome_verdict` is absent — exit code ≠ 0 on any violation.\n\n**KR3 — every verify-flow PR is review-ready or correctly auto-merged**\n\n* Baseline: PRs from drained tickets carry whatever body the worker produced — typically a one-line subject and no review guidance.\n* Target: 0 PRs submitted under the verify flow without either (a) a structured What / Why / Focus body or (b) an auto-merge label with reasoning.\n* Measured over: every PR submitted under the verify flow, for the project's duration.\n* How we'll know: the prep step writes its decision to `prep_verdict`; `drain-cycle grade` fails any verify-flow entry where `prep_verdict` is absent or claims a structured body the PR doesn't have.\n\n## Kill condition\n\nAfter the verify flow runs on 5+ hard tickets across 2 cycles, kill the contract if the Outcome Verifier produces zero true-positive findings — every halt a false positive, and verifier-passed Dones no better in PR review than default-flow drains. On kill: revert enforcement (milestone 3), keep the recording fields (cheap and useful), and record the negative result in `docs/design-decisions.md`.\n\n## Scope and context\n\n* **Design context:** `docs/architecture.md` (the two-layer split and the verdict-handoff seam) and `docs/design-decisions.md` §1 (the verifier-gated-Done supersession). This description is the source of truth for scope, KRs, and the kill condition.","url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/project/multi-agent-collaboration-for-correctness-5a36114c586c","createdAt":"2026-05-28T10:17:39.942Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:27:04.223Z","startedAt":"2026-06-01T06:52:26.789Z","completedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"startDate":"2026-06-01","startDateResolution":null,"targetDate":null,"targetDateResolution":null,"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"labels":[],"initiatives":[],"lead":{"id":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","name":"Anton Babushkin"},"status":{"id":"2941e5ab-173a-4b38-8032-c21209bb8883","name":"Backlog","type":"backlog"},"teams":[{"id":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","name":"Personal","key":"ABA"}],"milestones":[{"id":"a668c8fa-f026-4247-b3c5-c491c9865642","name":"2 — Record the pack's verdicts in the run log","description":"Every verify-flow run-log entry carries the verdicts the pack emits — `outcome_verdict`, `prep_verdict`, `responder_runs[]` — with matching OTel span attributes, each written from the artifact the skill leaves (reaching inside no skill).\n\nDone when: a verify-flow run writes the fields plus span attributes, and a default-flow run leaves them absent without error.","targetDate":null,"progress":"40%"},{"id":"36f3d280-d50c-49ed-a7f6-57e2f11e0e7a","name":"3 — Gate Done on the outcome verdict","description":"A verify-flow ticket reaches Done only with a recorded `outcome_verdict`; {"id":"ABA-320","title":"Walking skeleton: `verify`-labelled drain produces `flow: \"verify\"` in run log","description":"## What\n\nA ticket labelled `verify` in Linear drains through drain-cycle with `flow: \"verify\"` recorded in the run log entry; all other ticket drains continue to produce `flow: null`.\n\n## Why\n\nThis is the foundation that all five new roles write to and the grading commands read from. No other milestone can proceed without the system distinguishing which drains use the new flow.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* `labels.py` already handles `repo:` and `model:` parsing with an extensible pattern.\n* The run-log format can accommodate a new top-level string field without breaking existing readers.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* A `verify` label on a ticket that also has ambiguous or conflicting labels must not silently resolve — existing label-resolution error handling applies.\n* Existing run-log readers must not break on entries that gain a new field.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n* Draining a `verify`-labelled ticket produces a run-log entry with `flow: \"verify\"`.\n* Draining an unlabelled ticket produces `flow: null`.\n* All existing tests pass.\n* The `verify` label does not interfere with `repo:` or `model:` label resolution.","projectMilestone":{"id":"a7ee0bd3-8210-4993-888b-80fc05734476","name":"1 — Resolve the verify flow from a Linear {"id":"ABA-330","title":"Worker invokes PR Preparer after Outcome Verifier pass; `prep_verdict` written to run log","description":"## What\n\nThe drain-cycle worker session invokes `/shape:pr-prepare` after the Outcome Verifier returns pass and before the PR is submitted; `prep_verdict` is written to the run-log entry.\n\n## Why\n\nWithout this wiring, the PR Preparer skill exists but is never invoked — KR3 (every PR has a structured body or auto-merge label) cannot be satisfied mechanically.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* The drain-cycle worker has a defined PR submission step that can be preceded by the PR Preparer invocation.\n* The Graphite stack has already been created by the worker before PR Prepare runs.\n* If PR Preparer crashes or produces no verdict, the drain halts rather than submitting a bare PR.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* The ordering constraint (verifier pass → PR prep → PR submit) must be enforced; {"id":"ABA-328","title":"Halt-on-fail: `verify`-flow ticket failing Outcome Verifier halts cycle with worktree intact","description":"## What\n\nWhen the Outcome Verifier returns a fail verdict on a `verify`-flow ticket, the drain cycle halts, the worktree is left at its current state for operator inspection, and `cycle_halt_reason` in the run log names the specific findings.\n\n## Why\n\nWithout enforcement, the verifier's verdict is advisory — the worker can still mark Done on a fail. The halt path is what makes KR2 (zero silent Dones) mechanically true rather than policy-dependent.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* The existing halt machinery (design decisions §8/§9) provides the correct extension point.\n* `cycle_halt_reason` field either already exists or is added as part of this issue.\n* If the verifier crashes or times out, the halt fires by default (default-to-halt, not default-to-continue).\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* The halt must not trigger on non-verify-flow tickets; gate condition must be: `flow == \"verify\"` AND `outcome_verdict.result == \"fail\"`.\n* Leaving the worktree intact must not block subsequent drains of other tickets in the cycle.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n* A `verify`-labelled ticket where the Outcome Verifier returns fail: cycle halts; `cycle_halt_reason` names the failing findings; worktree is intact.\n* A `verify`-labelled ticket where the verifier returns pass: drain continues to Done normally.\n* A non-verify-flow ticket is unaffected.\n* `drain-cycle grade` can read the halted entry and identify it as a verifier-fail halt.","projectMilestone":{"id":"36f3d280-d50c-49ed-a7f6-57e2f11e0e7a","name":"3 — Gate Done on the outcome verdict"},"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-328/halt-on-fail-verify-flow-ticket-failing-outcome-verifier-halts-cycle","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-328-halt-on-fail-verify-flow-ticket-failing-outcome-verifier","createdAt":"2026-05-28T11:55:40.007Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T02:33:43.935Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Todo","statusType":"unstarted","labels":["drain-cycle","sonnet","high"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"stateHistory":[{"state":{"id":"b3776c54-764d-49bf-9c67-ea57a0a464e2","name":"Todo","type":"unstarted"},"startedAt":"2026-05-28T11:55:40.007Z","endedAt":null}],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","cycleId":"9e17380f-9527-40ef-8523-4e4358f12809","relations":{"blocks":[{"id":"ABA-334","title":"`drain-cycle grade --flow=verify`: reports pass-rate and exits nonzero on KR2 silent-Done violations"},{"id":"ABA-330","title":"Worker invokes PR Preparer after Outcome Verifier pass; {"id":"ABA-334","title":"`drain-cycle grade --flow=verify`: reports pass-rate and exits nonzero on KR2 silent-Done violations","description":"## What\n\n`drain-cycle grade --flow=verify` reads all grade files with `status: confirmed`, reports the KR1 pass-rate across the window, and exits nonzero if any entry has `flow == \"verify\"` AND `final_linear_state == \"completed\"` AND `outcome_verdict == null` (KR2 silent-Done violation).\n\n## Why\n\nThis is the grading command the operator runs to assess initiative health. Without it, KR grading is manual and KR2 enforcement is policy-dependent — the brake has no mechanical teeth.\n\n## Assumptions\n\n* Grade files with `status: draft` are excluded from the report but warned about.\n* The pass-rate denominator is the count of confirmed verify-flow entries in the measurement window.\n\n## Key Risks\n\n* If the run-log entry and the grade file are out of sync (e.g. run-log updated after confirmation), the command may produce a misleading result.\n* The KR2 detection logic must be exact: only `flow == \"verify\"` entries are checked; default-flow entries with missing verdicts are ignored.\n\n## Definition of Done\n\n* `drain-cycle grade --flow=verify` reports: total tickets graded, KR1 pass-rate, any KR2 violations by issue ID.\n* Any KR2 violation causes exit code ≠ 0.\n* Un-confirmed drafts are reported as warnings, not counted in the rate.\n* A run with no violations exits 0.","projectMilestone":{"id":"96358131-30f3-4cf1-a7fc-8bc90e1da590","name":"4 — Grade the outcome"},"priority":{"value":0,"name":"No priority"},"url":"https://linear.app/ababushkin/issue/ABA-334/drain-cycle-grade-flowverify-reports-pass-rate-and-exits-nonzero-on","gitBranchName":"anton/aba-334-drain-cycle-grade-flowverify-reports-pass-rate-and-exits","createdAt":"2026-05-28T11:57:00.088Z","updatedAt":"2026-06-15T13:20:47.659Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Todo","statusType":"unstarted","labels":["drain-cycle","sonnet","high"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"stateHistory":[{"state":{"id":"b3776c54-764d-49bf-9c67-ea57a0a464e2","name":"Todo","type":"unstarted"},"startedAt":"2026-05-28T11:57:00.088Z","endedAt":null}],"createdBy":"Anton Babushkin","createdById":"720d0535-dfc6-465f-8111-d1a3e6eab786","project":"Multi-agent collaboration for correctness","projectId":"812bc9b5-1618-48b8-8041-6d6223dc6d4c","team":"Personal","teamId":"c20f56da-f121-4653-bf6c-dc1c8aac21c1","cycleId":"9e17380f-9527-40ef-8523-4e4358f12809","relations":{"blocks":[{"id":"ABA-336","title":"Post-launch review: KR status recorded and initiative close decision documented"}],"blockedBy":[{"id":"ABA-333","title":"`drain-cycle grade-draft <issue>`: one-shot command writes per-ticket grade file with `status: draft`"},{"id":"ABA-328","title":"Halt-on-fail: `verify`-flow ticket failing Outcome Verifier halts cycle with worktree intact"}],"relatedTo":[],"duplicateOf":null}}
